# Graph Report - cfla  (2026-11-13)

## Corpus Check
- 11644 files · ~4,347,966 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 51835 nodes · 77257 edges · 9913 communities (7435 shown, 2478 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7688 edges (avg confidence: 0.78)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `536f315e`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
